# Credit terms: setup file for Claude sessions

This file is for a Claude session. A manual tester never needs it: each test case is complete on its own. It holds no environment address, credentials, cookies or session data. Generated by `authoring/render_setup.py` from the case sources; regenerate it whenever a case changes.

## 1. Header and sources
- Feature: Credit terms (epic SV-11638; stories SV-11670 to SV-11681). TestRail group 91454 (suite 1), sections in `sections.json`. 334 cases, drafted 23 Oct 2026, not in TestRail.
- PRD: PRD "Credit terms", Confluence 963346440, version 3 (22 Oct 2026, "Renamed to Credit terms", Chris Ward); saved `sources/CONFLUENCE-963346440-v3-2026-10-23.md` (1,075 lines, read in full 23 Oct 2026). Every quote is checked word for word against it by `authoring/build.py`.
- Jira: the epic and its 12 stories, saved `sources/JIRA-SV-11638-stories-2026-10-23.md`; sweep of 23 Oct 2026 in `sources/SWEEP-2026-10-23.md` (13 tickets, PRD still v3).
- Design: https://claude.ai/artifact/2erYxWz2ffDNcoXzEfu6wf ("Default Credit Terms"), saved in `design-2026-10-23/` and driven by a second worker; the design pass adds each case's pictures and design words under "References from Design".
- Tech plan: not yet shared (QA lead, 23 Oct 2026). A full pass follows when it arrives (Rule 134).
- Today's behaviour (facts, not expectations): `build/sv9087-credit-term-2026-08-27/FINDINGS.md` (Net 30 due dates, Credit Hold banner "Cannot invoice this customer. Customer is on Credit Hold", Charge Account withheld from COD, Invoice Date field on the Finance tab, vendor due dates on Accept Delivery) and `build/sv8815-customer-credit-2026-08-20/`.
- No QA branch for this epic is known yet. Every recipe below is UNVERIFIED until the build verification session runs it.
- At session start, ask the user for the site to use and how to sign in as each slot below. Never write a secret into this file.

## 3. Flags and settings
- No feature flag (PRD Key Decisions: "No feature flag."). The page is Settings > Finance > Credit Terms, below Payment Methods.
- Desktop browser, 1440 px wide unless the case names another width or a phone; light theme.
- Every case owns its records: example names carry ZZAUTOTEST and the case key, and term names stay within 31 characters.
- Shared test site: standard terms hold real customers (PRD §6: 1,528 on Due on Receipt), so a case that needs a term holding exactly N customers uses a fresh shop term made in its setup ({Term-A}); a case that must use a standard term states counts relatively ({Count-A} + 3) or reaches its customers in Move Customers with Select None, the search and a tick.
- Dates: the due-date cases set the Invoice Date on the Finance tab's invoice preview (existing behaviour, S7-R8); a case that needs "today" names it as {Date-A}.

## 4. Shared procedures (UNVERIFIED click paths; no build yet)
| Procedure | What | How | Check |
|---|---|---|---|
| P-TERM | One of the shop's own terms | Settings > Credit Terms > New Credit Term: the example name, the option and the number the case gives > Create Credit Term. Deactivate it from its row menu where the case says deactivated; make it the default with Make Default for New Customers where the case says default. | The row shows on the right tab with the Due text the case gives. |
| P-STD | Standard term state | Settings > Credit Terms: deactivate the named standard term from its row menu (or reactivate it from the Inactive tab). Make Default for New Customers on the term the case names as default (COD unless stated). | The tabs and the Default tag match the case. |
| P-CUST | Customer on a term | Customers > New Customer: the example name, Credit Terms and Credit Limit as the case gives > Save. For a blank or odd spelling ("NET 30", Prepaid), use the contacts import (P-IMPORT): the screen cannot store them. | The customer card's "Credit term" and credit limit match. |
| P-OLD | Data from before the release | Customers, vendors and invoices the case says are "from before the release" (a COD or Credit Hold customer with a limit above $0, a blank-term customer or vendor, a vendor invoice with no stored due date) are taken on production (QA lead, 26 Oct 2026: "This you can take from production environment if you need the BEFORE screenshots."): shortly before the release reaches production, find or make them on the production test account and capture the values and screenshots the case asks for; the case is then run on production after the release. After the release they cannot be made by hand. | Values written down with the date. |
| P-VENDOR | Vendor on a term | Parts > Vendors > New Vendor: the example name > Save & Close; then Edit Vendor > Credit Terms and Credit Limit as the case gives. | The vendor card's "Credit term" matches. |
| P-WO | Work order ready to invoice | Work Orders > New Work Order for the customer; add a labor line; approve; complete the Review step; leave it Complete (playbook: every part received with a part number, Review done, the customer has a contact). | Finance tab shows the invoice preview with Invoice Date and Due date, and Create Invoice is enabled. |
| P-INV | Invoice on a term | P-WO, then on the Finance tab set Invoice Date as the case gives and click Create Invoice; close the payment window unpaid unless the case says paid. For "held only by invoices", move the customer off the term afterwards with Move Customers. | The invoice shows the term and due date. |
| P-BAL | Balance over a limit | P-INV for the amounts the case names (unpaid), then set the credit limit in Edit Customer before the Over Credit check. | The customer's balance equals the sum of its unpaid invoices. |
| P-VINV | Vendor invoice | Parts > Vendors > Vendor Invoices: a new vendor invoice for the vendor with one part; set the invoice date as the case gives. For a delivery: create a purchase order for the vendor at the delivery's location and receive it there. | The vendor invoice shows the due date. |
| P-IMPORT | Contacts or vendor import | Settings > Data Import > Contacts (or Vendors): download the template, fill the rows the case names (name, credit term text, credit limit), upload. | The import finishes and the customers or vendors exist. |
| P-ROLE | Custom role and user | Settings > Roles & Permissions > Create custom role: Settings page access with only the sub-rows the case needs ("Finance" on or off), the "Customers" row's "Create & Edit" column and the "View and Manage AP/AR Data" cross toggle exactly as the case says; assign it to a test user (playbook §G: role swap on the test login, reset afterwards). Out-of-the-box roles: Reset to Template first (Rule 26). | The user signs in again (a role change signs the user out) and sees the expected menus. |
| P-TWO | Two browsers, two users | Sign in as the first Admin in one browser and as the second Admin ({User-B}) in another browser or a private window. | Both browsers show the starting screen. |
| P-LOC | Locations and time zones | Settings > Locations: {Location-A} (Edmonton time unless the case says otherwise), {Location-B} in another time zone the case names; a location with no time zone where the case asks (UNVERIFIED: the time zone may be required). | The location switcher lists both. |
| P-QBO | QuickBooks Online sandbox | Connect the test organization to the QuickBooks Online sandbox (playbook; build/sv9087-credit-term-2026-08-27/FINDINGS.md Check G: the qb1 environment, sync paused items, US tax codes). Sign in to the sandbox in another tab; Terms are under Settings > All lists > Terms. | Invoices sync and appear in the sandbox. |
| P-API | Public API | What the case's preconditions name: a valid Public API key for the test organization, the Public API documentation from the developers (the requests to create, update and read a customer) and a request tool such as Postman. The request addresses and field names are not in our sources yet (no tech plan); take them from that documentation. | UNVERIFIED until the documentation is in hand. |
| P-CLEAN | A shop holding only the case's own records | Where the case's preconditions say the shop has no other terms of its own, no deactivated term, or a log nobody else has written to, use a test organization that holds none of another session's terms, customers or log rows: a fresh test organization, or the shared one once every foreign term is reactivated and no foreign log row exists. Never reactivate or delete another session's terms on the shared site. | The tabs and the Credit Terms Log hold only what the case's preconditions name. |
| P-VOLUME | Large term | 2,000 or 2,500 customers on one term: contacts import with that many rows on the term (P-IMPORT), or Move Customers into it. | The term's Customers count equals the number. |

### SETUP-S1-03 — The Active tab lists terms in the fixed order, Credit Hold last
Section: Story 1 — Credit Terms page and list (TestRail section 91455). Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions with their placeholders and examples (for Claude sessions; the TestRail case states the same in plain words, without the examples):

- All twelve standard terms are active and COD is the default for new customers.
- The shop has five active terms of its own: {Term-A} due 10 days after the invoice date, {Term-B} due 30 days after the invoice date, {Term-C} due on day 1 of the following month, {Term-D} due on day 22 of the following month, and {Term-E} due on day 1 of the following month. {Term-E}'s name comes after {Term-C}'s in A to Z order. For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 Ten, {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 Thirty, {Term-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 First A, {Term-D} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 Twentysecond and {Term-E} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-03 First B.
- Other terms may also be listed; check only the order of the named rows.
- Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Preconditions as the TestRail case states them (tester read-through, 26 Oct 2026):

Term E's name comes after term C's in A to Z order.
  - Other terms may also be listed; check only the order of the named rows.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.

### SETUP-S2-12 — A shop term held only by invoices says how many invoices
Section: Story 2 — Row menu actions (TestRail section 91456). Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions with their placeholders and examples (for Claude sessions; the TestRail case states the same in plain words, without the examples):
- You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- COD is the shop's default for new customers.
- The shop has its own active term {Term-A} (made for this test), due 14 days after the invoice date, held by no customer and no vendor today, but by five invoices created while their customer was on it (the customer was then moved to Net 30). For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S2-12 Fourteen.
- Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Preconditions as the TestRail case states them (tester read-through, 26 Oct 2026):
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure COD is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Create a new term of the shop's own for this test, term A, due 14 days after the invoice date.
  - Create a customer for this test on term A, and create five invoices for that customer.
  - Then change that customer's credit term to Net 30, so term A is held by no customer or vendor, only by the five invoices.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Location-A} = the test location, {Term-A} = term A.
Placeholders: {Location-A}, {Term-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real numbers (work order, invoice) here after setup.

Section: Story 3 — Create or edit a credit term (TestRail section 91457). Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.

- Today's date on the calendar of {Location-A} is {Date-A}. For example: {Date-A} is Oct 21.
- Today's date on the calendar of {Location-B} is {Date-B}. For example: {Date-B} is Oct 22.

  - Note today's date on the calendar of the test location (date A).
  - Note today's date on the calendar of location B (date B).
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Date-A} = date A, {Date-B} = date B, {Location-A} = the test location, {Location-B} = location B.

### SETUP-S4-01 — Move Customers lists the term's customers A to Z, all ticked
Section: Story 4 — Move Customers (TestRail section 91458). Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions with their placeholders and examples (for Claude sessions; the TestRail case states the same in plain words, without the examples):
- You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- Net 30 is the shop's default for new customers.
- The shop's own term {Term-A}, due 15 days after the invoice date, is held by exactly three customers, {Customer-A}, {Customer-B} and {Customer-C} (a new term holds only this case's customers), whose names are in that A to Z order, with credit limits of $500.00, $0.00 and $1,000.00. For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 N15, {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 Alpha, {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 Bravo and {Customer-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S4-01 Charlie.
- Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Preconditions as the TestRail case states them (tester read-through, 26 Oct 2026):
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Make sure Net 30 is the shop's default for new customers.
  - Create a new term of the shop's own for this test, term A, due 15 days after the invoice date.
  - Create customer A, customer B and customer C for this test on term A, with names in that A to Z order and credit limits of $500.00, $0.00 and $1,000.00.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Customer-A} = customer A, {Customer-B} = customer B, {Customer-C} = customer C, {Location-A} = the test location, {Term-A} = term A.
Placeholders: {Customer-A}, {Customer-B}, {Customer-C}, {Location-A}, {Term-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real numbers (work order, invoice) here after setup.
Procedures: P-TERM, P-STD, P-CUST, P-INV.
Sources (verbatim text in section 9): S2-R1, S2-R1 MOVE, S4-R1, S4-R2, S4-R4, S4-R7, UFS-34.

Other terms of the shop may also be offered. For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S5-02 Ten, {Term-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S5-02 Fifth and {Term-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S5-02 Old.

- Your user name is {User-A}. For example: {User-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S12 Admin.
- You are enrolled at {Location-B}, in a time zone {Hours-A} hours apart from {Location-A}. For example: {Location-B} is in Toronto and {Hours-A} is 2 (Edmonton to Toronto).
- Net 50 is active.
- Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Preconditions as the TestRail case states them (tester read-through, 26 Oct 2026):
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Note your user name.
  - Make sure you are also enrolled at location B, in a time zone a whole number of hours apart from the test location, and note that difference in hours.
  - Make sure Net 50 is active.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Hours-A} = the difference in hours, {Location-A} = the test location, {Location-B} = location B, {User-A} = your user name.
Placeholders: {Hours-A}, {Location-A}, {Location-B}, {User-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real numbers (work order, invoice) here after setup.
Procedures: P-LOC.

### SETUP-DATA-18 — A vendor invoice from a delivery uses the delivery's location calendar
Section: Counts and due dates — exact numbers (TestRail section 91467). Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions with their placeholders and examples (for Claude sessions; the TestRail case states the same in plain words, without the examples):
- You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- Vendor {Vendor-A} (made for this test) holds Net 30. For example: {Vendor-A} is ZZAUTOTEST DATA-18 Vendor.
- A delivery for vendor {Vendor-A} belongs to {Location-B}, in a time zone where the date is already one day later than where you work. Run the case at a time of day when the two dates differ. For example: you work in Edmonton and {Location-B} is in Auckland.
- Today's date at {Location-B} is {Date-A}; {Date-B} is {Date-A} plus 30 days. For example: {Date-A} is Oct 22, 2026 and {Date-B} is Nov 21, 2026.
Preconditions as the TestRail case states them (tester read-through, 26 Oct 2026):
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Create vendor A for this test, on Net 30.
  - Have a delivery for vendor A, not yet received, at location B, a location whose time zone is already one day later than where you work.
  - Run the case at a time of day when the two dates differ.
  - Note today's date at location B (date A).
  - Work out date B: date A plus 30 days.
Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Date-A} = date A, {Date-B} = date B, {Location-A} = the test location, {Location-B} = location B, {Vendor-A} = vendor A.
Placeholders: {Date-A}, {Date-B}, {Location-A}, {Location-B}, {Vendor-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real numbers (work order, invoice) here after setup.
Procedures: P-VENDOR, P-INV, P-VINV, P-LOC.
Sources (verbatim text in section 9): S7-R6, S7-R6a.

Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Date-A} = date A, {Date-B} = date B, {Date-C} = date C, {Location-A} = the test location.

### SETUP-DATA-27 — Reversed and paid invoices count toward a term's holders, estimates never
Section: Counts and due dates — exact numbers (TestRail section 91467). Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions with their placeholders and examples (for Claude sessions; the TestRail case states the same in plain words, without the examples):
- You are an Admin user working at {Location-A}. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- The shop's own term {Term-A} is held by no customer or vendor today. It was used by three invoices (one paid, one reversed and one open) and by an estimate that was never invoiced; the customer was then moved to Net 30. For example: {Term-A} is ZZAUTOTEST DATA-27 Fourteen and the records belong to ZZAUTOTEST DATA-27 Customer.
- Starting screen: the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Preconditions as the TestRail case states them (tester read-through, 26 Oct 2026):
  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Create a new term of the shop's own for this test, term A, due 14 days after the invoice date.
  - Create a customer for this test on term A, with three invoices on term A (pay one, reverse one, leave one open) and an estimate that is never invoiced.
  - Then change that customer's credit term to Net 30, so term A is held by no customer or vendor.
  - Start on the Credit Terms page (Settings, then "Credit Terms" in the Finance group), Active tab.
Names in the TestRail case (Rule 117 fourteenth amendment): {Location-A} = the test location, {Term-A} = term A.
Placeholders: {Location-A}, {Term-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real numbers (work order, invoice) here after setup.
Procedures: P-TERM, P-INV.
Sources (verbatim text in section 9): S2-R2, T-INUSE.
