## 7. Dependencies and shared fixtures
- Cases marked `AUTOMATION: HOLD - needs the before values taken on production ...` need P-OLD done on production before the release reaches production.
- Cases that move customers off COD, Net 30 or other busy terms untick everyone first (Select None) and tick only the case's own customers.
- The QuickBooks cases share one sandbox; each uses its own term names so the Terms list never collides.

## 8. Reset and cleanup
- Leave ZZAUTOTEST records in place. Put the default back to what it was before the case (COD on an existing organization). Reactivate any standard term a case deactivated. Reset out-of-the-box roles to their templates and restore the test login's role (playbook §G).

## 9. Traceability — every source sentence the cases quote (PRD v3 unless marked Jira)
- **S1-E1**: "If every term that can be deactivated has been deactivated, the Active tab lists COD, Due on Receipt, Credit Hold and, when the default is another term, the default (it can't be deactivated, S2-N4). The Inactive tab lists everything else."
- **S1-E2**: "A term held only by invoices (no customer or vendor on it today) shows 0 in both columns but still counts as in use for Story 2: Row menu actions's locks."
- **S1-N1**: "If the user does not have the 'Settings → Finance' setting, they do not see "Credit Terms" under Settings, and opening the page's address directly does not show the page (same behavior as Payment Methods)."
- **S1-N2**: "If the Inactive tab has no terms, it shows the line "No inactive terms" in place of the table, and its count reads 0."
- **S1-N3**: "Every row has a "⋯" menu, because "Move Customers" appears on every row for every user who can open the page (S2-R1), greyed when it cannot be used. Credit Hold and the default COD row therefore always have a menu."
- **S1-R1**: "Under Settings, in the Finance group, a "Credit Terms" item appears directly below "Payment Methods". Selecting it opens the Credit Terms page."
- **S1-R10**: "No other term (standard Net terms and every term the shop created) shows an info icon or a lock icon."
- **S1-R11**: "The shop's default term shows a "Default" tag after its name (after the info and lock icons for COD and Due on Receipt). Exactly one row carries the tag."
- **S1-R12**: "The Customers column shows how many customers hold the term, right aligned, showing 0 when none. The Counter Sale customer is never counted. A customer with a blank term is counted under COD. Names that differ only in capitals and spaces count toward the same term ("NET 30" counts as Net 30; see §6)."
- **S1-R13**: "The Vendors column shows how many vendors hold the term, right aligned, 0 when none, by the same rules as S1-R12 (a blank vendor term counts under COD)."
- **S1-R14**: "The Active tab lists every active term; the Inactive tab lists every deactivated term, standard and the shop's own, in the same columns and order."
- **S1-R15**: "Term names are never cut off in the table; a long name wraps onto a second line, with its icons and "Default" tag after it."
- **S1-R16**: "The page is organization-wide. Switching location while on the page reloads it with the same terms, counts and default."
- **S1-R2**: "The page header shows the title "Credit Terms" and, top right, a primary "New Credit Term" button (Story 3: Create or edit a credit term) with a "⋯" page menu beside it (Story 12: Credit Terms audit log)."
- **S1-R3**: "Below the header are two tabs, "Active" and "Inactive", each followed by a count of the terms in it. The page opens on Active."
- **S1-R4**: "Each tab shows one table with the columns Name, Due, Customers, Vendors, and an unlabeled actions column holding the row's "⋯" menu (Story 2: Row menu actions). The columns keep the same widths on both tabs."
- **S1-R5**: "Rows are listed in this order on both tabs: COD; Due on Receipt; every Net term (standard and the shop's own) by number of days, lowest first; every "day of the following month" term by day, lowest first; any other term A to Z; Credit Hold last. Ties sort A to Z by name. The same order is used in every customer and vendor Credit Terms dropdown."
- **S1-R6**: "Clicking a column header sorts the table by that column, following the app-wide sorting rule: the first click sorts Name A to Z, Customers and Vendors lowest first, and Due in the S1-R5 order; each further click reverses the direction. The sort is not saved: leaving and returning to the page shows the S1-R5 order."
- **S1-R7**: "The Due column describes each term in words:"
- **S1-R7 CH** (S1-R7, Credit Hold): "Credit Hold: "Invoicing is blocked""
- **S1-R7 COD** (S1-R7, COD): "COD: "Paid when the invoice is created""
- **S1-R7 DAY** (S1-R7, a day of the following month term): "A "day of the following month" term: "{ordinal day} of the following month", for example "10th of the following month", "1st of the following month", "22nd of the following month""
- **S1-R7 DOR** (S1-R7, Due on Receipt): "Due on Receipt: "Due on the invoice date""
- **S1-R7 NET** (S1-R7, a Net term): "A Net term: "{X} days after the invoice date" ("1 day after the invoice date" when X is 1), for example "30 days after the invoice date""
- **S1-R8**: "COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold each show, after the name, an info icon and a lock icon. Hovering the info icon shows:"
- **S1-R8 CH** (S1-R8, Credit Hold info icon): "Credit Hold: "No invoices can be created for a customer on Credit Hold until their credit term is changed.""
- **S1-R8 COD** (S1-R8, COD info icon): "COD: "Cash on delivery. COD customers can't charge to account, so payment is taken when the invoice is created.""
- **S1-R8 DOR** (S1-R8, Due on Receipt info icon): "Due on Receipt: "Payment is due on the invoice date. You can charge the invoice to the customer's account instead of taking payment.""
- **S1-R9**: "Hovering the lock icon shows "Always available." with the line "COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold can't be deactivated." below it."
- **S10-E1**: "A name that matches a deactivated term is saved as that term; the customer then shows it as "{name} (Inactive)" (S5-R8)."
- **S10-E2**: "Credit limits sent with an import or API customer are handled as today, except as S10-R6 and S10-R8 state. Both apply only to customers created or updated after release; an existing COD or Credit Hold customer's stored limit is never changed until that customer is next created or updated by one of those paths (S11-R11)."
- **S10-E3**: "When an import updates an existing customer or vendor, a blank credit term cell keeps the stored term."
- **S10-N1**: "Imports and the Public API never refuse a row or a request because of its credit term."
- **S10-N2**: "Vendors imported with a blank term start on COD, not the default (S6-E1)."
- **S10-R1**: "A customer created by the contacts import with a blank credit term gets the shop's default."
- **S10-R2**: "A customer created through the Public API without a credit term, or with a blank one, gets the shop's default."
- **S10-R3**: "A credit term that matches one of the shop's terms (standard or its own) ignoring capitals and spaces is saved in that term's spelling: "net 10", "NET10" and "Net 10" all save as the shop's "Net 10". This applies to customers and vendors created or updated by an import, and created or updated through the Public API."
- **S10-R4**: "Any other text is saved exactly as sent and dates invoices as it does today (lenient, as today)."
- **S10-R5**: "Updating a customer's credit term through the Public API follows S10-R3 and S10-R4."
- **S10-R6**: "A new customer created by the contacts import on COD or Credit Hold gets a $0 credit limit, whatever limit the file holds."
- **S10-R7**: "The Public API reads, creates and updates a customer's credit term. It has no way to list the shop's terms or its default; an integrator sends a term name and reads back the saved spelling (S10-R3). The vendor API is unchanged."
- **S10-R8**: "A customer created or updated through the Public API, or updated by the contacts import, onto COD or Credit Hold is saved with a $0 credit limit, whatever limit was sent; the request or row is not refused (S10-N1). A Public API update that sends neither a credit term nor a credit limit leaves the stored limit as it is."
- **S11-E1**: "An organization with no settings record yet behaves as COD (the pre-release behavior) until a default is picked."
- **S11-E2**: "Customers whose stored term has an odd spelling (for example "NET 30") keep that stored text; they count under the matching term on the page and show their stored text on the card."
- **S11-N1**: "Nothing is moved, re-dated or re-sent to QuickBooks at release."
- **S11-N2**: "Users without 'Settings → Finance' see no new Settings item; users without 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' see no new customer fields."
- **S11-R1**: "Every organization that exists at release keeps COD as its default for new customers. Nothing changes for new customers until a user picks another default."
- **S11-R10**: "COD behaves as today at invoicing: Create Invoice still opens the payment window, Charge Account is still hidden for COD customers, and closing the window without paying still removes the new invoice. The COD row's Due text and info tooltip describe that existing behavior; they do not change it. The payment window opens for every term at invoicing, as today; COD is the only term whose window has no Charge Account."
- **S11-R11**: "An existing COD or Credit Hold customer with a credit limit above $0 keeps that limit on release day."
- **S11-R12**: "An organization created after release starts with Due on Receipt as its default."
- **S11-R13**: "Due dates ShopView calculates after release use the calendar of the record's own location (S7-R6); due dates stored on existing invoices and vendor invoices are never rewritten (S7-E2)."
- **S11-R14**: "Screens that recalculate a due date from the term rather than showing a stored due date (the A/R dashboard for records with no stored due date, and credit memo due dates on statements and the unpaid transactions list) can show a date one day later than they showed before release, for a term whose span crosses a daylight-saving change. The later date is the correct one; it is the same fix as SV-11414. Stored due dates are never rewritten. The Vendor Bill Export, which today recalculates from the vendor's current term, shows each vendor invoice's stored due date from release (S7-R11), so after a vendor's term changes it still matches the vendor invoice."
- **S11-R16**: "The first time such a customer (S11-R11) is edited, Edit Customer shows $0.00, disabled, and saving saves $0 (S5-R9)."
- **S11-R17**: "If such a customer is moved to COD or Credit Hold with Move Customers, its limit becomes $0 at that moment, after the S4-R14 confirmation, and its previous limit is kept in its note (S4-R20)."
- **S11-R2**: "No existing customer, vendor or invoice changes term, credit limit or due date at release. The Counter Sale customer stays on COD permanently."
- **S11-R3**: "All twelve standard terms are active at release, and no organization has its own terms."
- **S11-R4**: "The Credit Terms page appears under Settings → Finance for every user with 'Settings → Finance'."
- **S11-R5**: "New Customer now shows Credit Terms (prefilled with the default, COD for existing shops) and Credit Limit to users with 'View and Manage AP/AR Data'. Users without it see New Customer as before."
- **S11-R6**: "New Customer and Edit Customer no longer show "Customer name is a required field" as soon as the window opens and another field is clicked, which showed an error before the user had typed anything. The name is checked once the user has typed in it and leaves the field, or when they press Save with no name."
- **S11-R7**: "New Vendor and Edit Vendor no longer show "Name is a required field" as soon as the window opens and another field is clicked, which showed an error before the user had typed anything. The name is checked once the user has typed in it and leaves the field, or when they press Save & Close with no name."
- **S11-R8**: "The Over Credit warning and the "Over limit" badge stop showing for customers with a $0 or blank credit limit and for customers on COD, Due on Receipt or Credit Hold (Story 9: Over Credit warning)."
- **S11-R9**: "Customers and vendors with a blank term are counted under COD on the Credit Terms page; their records are not changed."
- **S12-E1**: "When the log has no rows, the table shows the log's standard empty state, "No history here. Yet.""
- **S12-E2**: "A deleted term's earlier rows (created, edited, deactivated) stay in the log under its name."
- **S12-E3**: "A move where some ticked customers had already left the term (S4-E1) logs the number actually moved."
- **S12-N1**: "If the user does not have the 'Settings → Finance' setting, they cannot reach the page or its "⋯" menu, and a request for the log is refused by the server."
- **S12-N2**: "Changes made before release are not in the log; it starts empty on release day."
- **S12-N3**: "Changes made outside the Credit Terms page (a customer's or vendor's term or credit limit changed in New Customer, Edit Customer, Edit Vendor, by import or by the Public API) are not in this log."
- **S12-R1**: "The Credit Terms page header has a "⋯" page menu top right, beside "New Credit Term", with one item: "Audit Log"."
- **S12-R2**: ""Audit Log" opens a window titled "Credit Terms Log" with the same layout as the Work Order Log and the Part Sale Log: a search box, then a table with the columns Event, User, Details, Date and Time, newest first."
- **S12-R3**: "Each change saved after release adds one row. There is no Line column. "{rule}" is the term's Due column text (S1-R7). The Event and Details columns read:"
- **S12-R3 CREATED** (S12-R3 table, created): "A term is created (Story 3: Create or edit a credit term) | "Credit term created" | "{name}: {rule}", for example "Net 14: 14 days after the invoice date""
- **S12-R3 DEACT** (S12-R3 table, deactivated): "A term is deactivated (Story 2: Row menu actions) | "Credit term deactivated" | "{name}: {rule}""
- **S12-R3 DEF** (S12-R3 table, default changed): "The default is changed (Story 2: Row menu actions) | "Default changed" | "{old} → {new}", for example "COD → Net 14""
- **S12-R3 DEL** (S12-R3 table, deleted): "A term is deleted (Story 2: Row menu actions) | "Credit term deleted" | "{name}: {rule}""
- **S12-R3 EDITED** (S12-R3 table, edited): "An unused term is edited (Story 3: Create or edit a credit term) | "Credit term edited" | Only what changed: "Name: {old} → {new}" and/or "Due: {old rule} → {new rule}""
- **S12-R3 HEAD** (S12-R3 table, header row): "Change | Event | Details"
- **S12-R3 MOVED** (S12-R3 table, customers moved): "Customers are moved (Story 4: Move Customers) | "Customers moved" | "{n} customers from {from} to {to}" ("1 customer from {from} to {to}" for one), followed by "Credit limit set to $0 (previous limits are in each customer's note)" when the target is COD or Credit Hold"
- **S12-R3 REACT** (S12-R3 table, reactivated): "A term is reactivated (Story 2: Row menu actions) | "Credit term reactivated" | "{name}: {rule}""
- **S12-R4**: "The User column shows the name of the user who made the change; Date and Time show when, on the calendar of the location the user is working in."
- **S12-R5**: "Typing in the search box narrows the table to rows whose Event, User or Details text contains the search text, ignoring capitals."
- **S12-R6**: "The log is organization-wide (one log for all locations), kept indefinitely, and cannot be edited or deleted by anyone."
- **S12-R7**: "A row is written only when a change is saved. A refused or canceled action (the error handling of Stories 2, 3 and 4, or Cancel) writes nothing, and neither does an action that changes nothing (for example making the current default the default again)."
- **S12-R8**: "Event, User and Details text is never cut off; long names wrap."
- **S2-E1**: "Editing the name of an unused term that is the current default keeps it the default under its new name."
- **S2-E2**: "If a term becomes in use, becomes the default, or is changed by another user after the page loaded, the save is refused with the reason in S2 Error Handling, and the page keeps its state."
- **S2-E3**: "Deactivating a standard term in use (for example Net 30 with 290 customers) is allowed; those customers keep Net 30, shown "Net 30 (Inactive)" on their card and in Edit Customer."
- **S2-N1**: "Standard terms never show "Edit" or "Delete Credit Term"."
- **S2-N2**: "COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold never show "Deactivate" (S1-R9 explains why on the lock icon)."
- **S2-N3**: "Credit Hold never shows "Make Default for New Customers"."
- **S2-N4**: "The current default never shows "Make Default for New Customers"; its "Deactivate" and, for a term the shop created, "Delete Credit Term" show blocked with the default reason (S2-R1)."
- **S2-N5**: "A term in use shows "Edit" and "Delete Credit Term" blocked with the "Used by ..." reason; "Deactivate" stays available."
- **S2-N6**: "Clicking or pressing Enter on a blocked item does nothing and keeps the menu open."
- **S2-R1**: "Each row's "⋯" menu shows only the items that apply to that term, in this order. An item that applies but is blocked shows greyed out and does nothing when clicked; hovering it shows the reason."
- **S2-R1 DEACT** (S2-R1 table, Deactivate row): ""Deactivate" (archive) | Active terms except COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold | The term is the default: "This is your default for new customers" with "Pick another default first." below"
- **S2-R1 DEL** (S2-R1 table, Delete Credit Term row): ""Delete Credit Term" (trash, red, last, below a divider) | Terms the shop created, active or inactive | The term is the default: "This is your default for new customers" / "Pick another default first."; otherwise the term is in use: "Used by {holders}" / "Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers.""
- **S2-R1 EDIT** (S2-R1 table, Edit row): ""Edit" (pencil) | Active terms the shop created | The term is in use: "Used by {holders}" with "Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." below"
- **S2-R1 HEAD** (S2-R1 table, header row): "Item (icon) | Shown on | Blocked when (hover text)"
- **S2-R1 MD** (S2-R1 table, Make Default row): ""Make Default for New Customers" (circle check) | Active terms that are not the default and not Credit Hold | Never"
- **S2-R1 MOVE** (S2-R1 table, Move Customers row): ""Move Customers" (person) | Every term, active or inactive | The user lacks 'Customers → Create & Edit' or 'View and Manage AP/AR Data': "You need Customers → Create & Edit and View and Manage AP/AR Data to move customers." Otherwise no customer holds the term: "No customers are on this term""
- **S2-R1 REACT** (S2-R1 table, Reactivate row): ""Reactivate" (rotate) | Inactive terms; on the Inactive tab it comes before "Move Customers" | Never"
- **S2-R10**: "Make Default is not available on inactive terms (it is absent from their menu)."
- **S2-R11**: "The confirm button in the Make Default and Delete confirmations is disabled while the change is saving, so a double click saves once."
- **S2-R12**: "Every success toast that shows a term name ({name} or {term}) on the Credit Terms page, in Story 3: Create or edit a credit term and in Story 4: Move Customers shows the full name; a long name wraps and is never cut off."
- **S2-R2**: ""{holders}" names only the holder types the term has, each with its count, singular or plural: for example "Used by 1 customer and 2 vendors", "Used by 12 customers, 3 vendors and 40 invoices", "Used by 5 invoices"."
- **S2-R3**: "Hovering "Deactivate" (when not blocked) shows "Hide from customer and vendor pickers". Hovering "Reactivate" shows "Show in pickers again"."
- **S2-R4**: ""Edit" opens the "Edit Credit Term" window (Story 3: Create or edit a credit term) filled with the term's name, kind and days."
- **S2-R5**: ""Make Default for New Customers" opens a confirmation titled "Make {name} the default?" with the message "New customers start on {name}. Customers you already have keep their current term." and the buttons "Cancel" and "Make Default"."
- **S2-R6**: "On "Make Default", the term becomes the default, the "Default" tag moves to its row, and a success toast shows "{name} is now the default" with the line "New customers start on {name}." No customer, vendor or invoice changes."
- **S2-R7**: ""Deactivate" acts at once, without a confirmation: the term moves to the Inactive tab, both tab counts update, and a success toast shows "{name} deactivated". Every customer and vendor on the term keeps it (Story 5: New Customer and Edit Customer, Story 6: Vendors)."
- **S2-R8**: ""Reactivate" acts at once: the term moves back to the Active tab, the counts update, it appears in the customer and vendor pickers again, and a success toast shows "{name} reactivated"."
- **S2-R9**: ""Delete Credit Term" opens a confirmation titled "Confirmation" with the message "Are you sure you want to delete this credit term?" and the buttons "Cancel" and a red "Delete Credit Term". On confirm the term is removed from the page and a success toast shows "{name} deleted"."
- **S3-E1**: "A name that reads like a standard term but is not one (for example "Net 14", "Net 10", "Net 6") is allowed. The name and the rule are independent: a term named "Net 6" may be due on the 25th of the following month. The Due column always shows the rule."
- **S3-E2**: "If another user creates a term with the same name while the window is open, Create is refused with "A term called {name} already exists." and the window stays open."
- **S3-N1**: "If the user does not have the 'Settings → Finance' setting, they cannot reach the page and do not see "New Credit Term"."
- **S3-N2**: "If the term is in use, standard, or inactive, Edit is not offered or is blocked (Story 2: Row menu actions), so the window cannot open in Edit mode for it."
- **S3-N3**: ""Cancel" or the close icon closes the window without saving and clears its contents; reopening "New Credit Term" starts empty."
- **S3-R1**: ""New Credit Term" opens a window titled "New Credit Term"; Edit opens the same window titled "Edit Credit Term". The buttons are "Cancel" and "Create Credit Term" (New) or "Save Changes" (Edit)."
- **S3-R10**: "The Name is checked only once the user has typed in it (then each time they leave the field) or presses Create / Save. Opening the window, or clicking an option or a box before typing a name, never shows a Name error."
- **S3-R11**: "The chosen box re-checks on every change: correcting an invalid number (for example 0 to 25 in the Day box) clears its error immediately, without leaving the box."
- **S3-R12**: "Leading and trailing spaces in the Name are removed, and runs of spaces become one space, before the name is saved."
- **S3-R13**: "Editing a term keeps its own name valid (saving "Net 10" unchanged on the term "Net 10" is not a duplicate)."
- **S3-R14**: ""Create Credit Term" and "Save Changes" are disabled while saving, so a double click saves once."
- **S3-R15**: "Pressing Escape or clicking outside the window does not close it, and keeps everything typed."
- **S3-R16**: "The example date in S3-R5 is today on the calendar of the location the user is working in; after a location switch it follows the new location."
- **S3-R2**: "The window holds:"
- **S3-R2 NAME** (S3-R2, Name): "Name (required), focused on open, accepting at most 31 characters, with the hint "Up to 31 characters. Prints on invoices and goes to QuickBooks.""
- **S3-R2 OPTS** (S3-R2, the two options): "Two options, one chosen at a time: "Due Net X days" with a Days box followed by "days after the invoice date", and "Due day of the following month" with a Day box followed by "of the following month"."
- **S3-R3**: "A new term opens on "Due Net X days" with both boxes empty and the Day box disabled. Only the chosen option's box is active; the other is disabled and empty."
- **S3-R4**: "Clicking the disabled box chooses its option and puts the cursor in it. Switching options empties the box being left, so only the chosen option's number is checked and saved."
- **S3-R5**: "While the chosen box holds a valid number, a blue information line below the options explains the term, using today's date as the example invoice date (S3-R16):"
- **S3-R5 DAY** (S3-R5, day of the following month line): "Day of the following month: "{Name} means payment is due on the {ordinal X} of the following month. An invoice dated {today} is due {due}." For example: "10th of next month means payment is due on the 10th of the following month. An invoice dated Oct 21 is due Nov 10.""
- **S3-R5 EMPTY** (S3-R5, empty name and dates): "With the Name empty, "{Name}" reads "This term". Dates read as a short month and day ("Oct 21"). The line updates as the user types and disappears while the number is invalid."
- **S3-R5 NET** (S3-R5, Net line): "Net: "{Name} means payment is due {X} days after the invoice date. An invoice dated {today} is due {due}." For example: "Net 14 Days means payment is due 14 days after the invoice date. An invoice dated Oct 21 is due Nov 4.""
- **S3-R6**: ""Create Credit Term" / "Save Changes" stays disabled until the Name and the chosen box are both valid."
- **S3-R7**: "On Create, the window closes, a success toast shows "{name} created", and the term appears on the Active tab in its S1-R5 position, with 0 customers and 0 vendors, available at once in every customer and vendor Credit Terms dropdown."
- **S3-R8**: "On Save Changes, the window closes, a success toast shows "{name} saved", and the row shows the new name and Due text."
- **S3-R9**: "Validation rules and their exact messages, shown under the field:"
- **S3-R9 DAY** (S3-R9, Day): "Day not a whole number from 1 to 31: "Enter a day from 1 to 31.""
- **S3-R9 DAYS** (S3-R9, Days): "Days not a whole number from 1 to 365: "Enter 1 to 365 days.""
- **S3-R9 DUP** (S3-R9, another of the shop's terms): "Name matches another of the shop's terms, active or inactive, ignoring capitals and spaces: "A term called {existing name} already exists.""
- **S3-R9 EMPTY** (S3-R9, Name empty): "Name empty: "Enter a name.""
- **S3-R9 LONG** (S3-R9, 31 characters): "The Name field stops accepting characters after the 31st. A longer name reaching the server is refused with "Use 31 characters or fewer.""
- **S3-R9 STD** (S3-R9, a standard term's name): "Name matches a standard term ignoring capitals and spaces (for example "net 30" or "Net30"): "{Standard name} is already a standard term. Pick another name." (for example "Net 30 is already a standard term. Pick another name.")"
- **S4-E1**: "A customer ticked in the window whose term another user changed meanwhile is left alone and not counted in the toast."
- **S4-E2**: "Moving customers to Credit Hold is allowed; from then on no invoice can be created for them until their term changes (existing Credit Hold behavior)."
- **S4-E3**: "Moving customers to COD or Credit Hold sets each moved customer's credit limit to $0 (S4-R16), whatever limit they had, and keeps the previous limit in each note (S4-R20). Moving customers off COD or Credit Hold to another term leaves their $0 limit as it is until someone enters a new one in Edit Customer."
- **S4-E4**: "Moving customers off a deactivated term empties it; once no customer, vendor or invoice holds a term the shop created, it can be deleted."
- **S4-N1**: "If the user has 'Settings → Finance' but lacks 'Customers → Create & Edit' or 'View and Manage AP/AR Data', "Move Customers" shows greyed in every row menu with the hover text "You need Customers → Create & Edit and View and Manage AP/AR Data to move customers." A move sent by other means without all three permissions is refused by the server."
- **S4-N2**: "If no customer is on the term, "Move Customers" shows greyed with the hover text "No customers are on this term"."
- **S4-N3**: "Inactive terms and Credit Hold are valid sources (Move Customers is how a shop empties a deactivated term). Inactive terms are never offered in "Move To"."
- **S4-R1**: ""Move Customers" in a term's row menu (Story 2: Row menu actions) opens a window titled "Move Customers from {term}"."
- **S4-R10**: "When "Move To" is COD or Credit Hold, the window shows the line "Their credit limit will be set to $0." below "Move To"."
- **S4-R11**: "Each moved customer gets a note on its Notes, authored by the user who moved it, reading "Credit term changed from {old term} to {new term} by {user name} (Move Customers)." A customer moved from a blank term reads "from COD"."
- **S4-R12**: "Each moved customer gets the new term (and, under S4-R16, a $0 credit limit) and the S4-R11 note. AccountingHub receives the same customer update an Edit Customer save sends. The customer's other fields are not re-checked, so a customer with incomplete details still moves. No customer update is sent to QuickBooks (S8-R6)."
- **S4-R13**: "For a term with up to 2,000 customers, the window shows its full list in under 1 second, and the list scrolls through every customer without paging."
- **S4-R14**: "When "Move To" is COD or Credit Hold, pressing "Move {n} Customers" first opens a confirmation titled "Set {n} customers' credit limit to $0?" ("Set 1 customer's credit limit to $0?" for one), with the message "This can't be undone. Their current limits are kept in each customer's note, and the Credit Terms Log records the move." and the buttons "Cancel" and a red "Set Limits to $0 and Move" (displayed as "Set Limits To $0 And Move")."
- **S4-R15**: ""Cancel" in that confirmation returns to the Move Customers window with the search, ticks and "Move To" choice kept."
- **S4-R16**: "On "Set Limits to $0 and Move", each moved customer's credit limit is set to $0 along with the term."
- **S4-R17**: "When "Move To" is any other term, no confirmation opens, and each moved customer's credit limit stays as it was."
- **S4-R18**: "Every field other than the credit term (and, under S4-R16, the credit limit) stays as it was on each moved customer."
- **S4-R19**: "Customers not ticked, and customers on other terms, are untouched."
- **S4-R2**: "The window shows, top to bottom: a "Search customers" box; "Select All" and "Select None" with "{n} selected" on the right; a scrolling list of every customer on the term, A to Z, each with a checkbox; a "Move To" dropdown; and the buttons "Cancel" and "Move {n} Customers" ("Move 1 Customer" when one is selected)."
- **S4-R20**: "When a move changes a customer's limit (a limit above $0 set to $0), that customer's note (S4-R11) ends with "Credit limit {old limit} → $0." A customer whose limit was already $0 or blank gets the S4-R11 note without it."
- **S4-R21**: ""Move {n} Customers" and "Set Limits to $0 and Move" are disabled while the move is saving, so a double click moves once."
- **S4-R22**: "Pressing Escape or clicking outside the Move Customers window does not close it, and keeps the search, ticks and "Move To" choice. "Cancel" or the close icon closes it without moving anyone."
- **S4-R23**: "Customer names and term names are never cut off in the window title, the list or "Move To"; long names wrap."
- **S4-R24**: "One move has no customer limit. For a term with more than 2,000 customers the window still lists every customer without paging; the S4-R13 one-second target applies up to 2,000."
- **S4-R3**: "The list holds every customer whose term matches this term ignoring capitals and spaces. On COD it also holds customers with a blank term. The Counter Sale customer is never listed."
- **S4-R4**: "Every listed customer starts ticked."
- **S4-R5**: "Typing in the search box narrows the list to customers whose name contains the text, ignoring capitals. Pressing Enter in the search box does not move anyone. When nothing matches, the list area shows "No customers match your search.""
- **S4-R6**: ""Select All" ticks, and "Select None" unticks, every customer the search currently matches, including those not scrolled into view. Customers hidden by the search keep their ticks."
- **S4-R7**: ""Move To" offers every active term except the one being moved from, in the S1-R5 order. It is prefilled with the shop's default; while the default is selected, the hint "Prefilled with your default for new customers." shows below it. When the term being moved from is the default, "Move To" starts empty."
- **S4-R8**: ""Move {n} Customers" is disabled while no customer is ticked or no term is chosen in "Move To"."
- **S4-R9**: "On confirm, each ticked customer's credit term changes to the chosen term, the window closes, the page's Customers counts update, and a success toast shows "{n} customers moved to {term}." ("1 customer moved to {term}." for one)."
- **S5-E1**: "If the default changes while New Customer is open, the form keeps the term already shown; the user's Save uses what the form shows."
- **S5-E2**: "A customer whose stored term is text ShopView does not know (from an old import) shows that text selected in Edit Customer, after the active terms, without "(Inactive)"."
- **S5-N1**: "If the user does not have the 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' setting, New Customer and Edit Customer show neither Credit Terms nor Credit Limit (as Edit Customer today)."
- **S5-N2**: "Inactive terms are never offered to a customer not already on them."
- **S5-N3**: "Credit Hold is offered in the dropdown (as today) but never carries the "Default" tag, because it can never be the default."
- **S5-N4**: "If the user does not have the 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' setting, the server ignores any term or limit sent with their save: a customer they create gets the shop's default term, with a $0 limit when the default is COD and no limit otherwise; a customer they edit keeps its stored term and limit."
- **S5-R1**: "New Customer shows a "Credit Terms" dropdown and a "Credit Limit" field side by side, in the same place they sit on Edit Customer."
- **S5-R10**: "The Name field on New Customer and Edit Customer is checked only once the user has typed in it (then each time they leave it) or presses Save with no name, because it used to show an error before the user had typed anything, as soon as they clicked another field such as Credit Terms. See Story 11: Release day (S11-R6)."
- **S5-R11**: "A term name of up to 31 characters prints in full, without clipping, on modern and legacy invoices and estimates. Work order prints do not show the credit term."
- **S5-R12**: "The inactive term stays selected until the user picks another term."
- **S5-R13**: "Once another term is picked and saved, the inactive term is no longer offered to that customer."
- **S5-R14**: "Term names are never cut off in the Credit Terms dropdown or on the customer card, including with "(Inactive)" or the "Default" tag; long names wrap."
- **S5-R15**: "Every customer created in the app (from the Customers page, New Work Order and New Part Sale) is created in this New Customer window, so this story covers all of them. Imports and the Public API are the only other ways to create a customer (Story 10: Imports and Public API); the Counter Sale customer is created with the organization and always on COD."
- **S5-R2**: "On New Customer, Credit Terms is prefilled with the shop's default."
- **S5-R3**: "The Credit Terms dropdown on New Customer and Edit Customer lists the shop's active terms in the S1-R5 order. The default carries a "Default" tag after its name."
- **S5-R4**: "When the chosen term is COD or Credit Hold, Credit Limit shows $0.00 and is disabled; hovering it shows "COD and Credit Hold customers can't charge to account, so they have no credit limit." Choosing COD or Credit Hold sets the limit to $0.00."
- **S5-R5**: "For any other term, Credit Limit is editable, as on Edit Customer today. Switching from COD or Credit Hold to another term leaves the limit at $0.00 until the user enters one."
- **S5-R6**: "On Save, the customer is created with the chosen term and limit."
- **S5-R7**: "On Edit Customer, a customer whose term was deactivated shows it as "{name} (Inactive)" in the dropdown, selected, listed after the active terms."
- **S5-R8**: "The customer card's "Credit term" shows "{name} (Inactive)" for a deactivated term. Invoices and printed documents show the plain name."
- **S5-R9**: "Opening Edit Customer for a COD or Credit Hold customer that has a credit limit stored from before release shows $0.00, disabled; saving the customer saves $0."
- **S6-E1**: "A vendor imported with a blank term starts on COD, as today; a vendor imported with a known term in any capitalization is saved in that term's correct spelling."
- **S6-E2**: "Vendors on a term count toward its "in use" lock (Story 2: Row menu actions) and its Vendors column (Story 1: Credit Terms page and list)."
- **S6-N1**: "If the user does not have the 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' setting, the vendor Credit Terms field stays hidden, as today."
- **S6-N2**: "Inactive terms are never offered to a vendor not already on them."
- **S6-R1**: "The Credit Terms dropdown on New Vendor and Edit Vendor lists the shop's active terms, standard and its own, in the S1-R5 order."
- **S6-R2**: "New Vendor starts on COD, as today. The shop's default does not apply to vendors, and the vendor dropdown shows no "Default" tag."
- **S6-R3**: "A vendor on a deactivated term shows "{name} (Inactive)" on the vendor card's "Credit term" and selected in Edit Vendor, listed after the active terms, until someone picks another."
- **S6-R4**: "Vendor credit limits behave exactly as today on every term, COD and Credit Hold included: no lock, no $0 rule."
- **S6-R5**: "The Name field on New Vendor and Edit Vendor is checked only once the user has typed in it (then each time they leave it) or presses Save & Close with no name, because it used to show an error before the user had typed anything. See Story 11: Release day (S11-R7)."
- **S6-R6**: "Term names are never cut off in the vendor Credit Terms dropdown or on the vendor card, including with "(Inactive)"; long names wrap."
- **S7-E1**: "A deactivated term still dates invoices and vendor invoices for the customers and vendors that keep it."
- **S7-E2**: "Due dates already stored on existing invoices and vendor invoices are never rewritten. A screen that recalculates a due date from the customer's term shows the date on the calendar of the transaction's own location (S7-R6, S11-R14)."
- **S7-E3**: "An invoice created after a reversal takes the customer's term at that moment, as today: a deactivated term the customer still holds, or the new term after a move or edit. The reversed invoice's old due date does not come back."
- **S7-E4**: "If the customer on an open work order or part sale is changed, the invoice takes the new customer's term at invoicing, by the same rule. Nothing about the term is copied onto the work order or part sale before invoicing."
- **S7-N1**: "A term whose text ShopView does not recognize (from an old import or the Public API) is dated exactly as today."
- **S7-R1**: "A Net term (standard or the shop's own) is due that many days after the invoice date: an invoice dated Oct 5 on Net 30 is due Nov 4; on a shop's "Net 10" it is due Oct 15."
- **S7-R10**: "A Net 30 invoice or vendor invoice whose span crosses the March or November clock change is due on the 30th calendar day after its date."
- **S7-R11**: "The Vendor Bill Export shows each vendor invoice's stored due date (S7-R9), never one recalculated from the vendor's current term; it is blank when no due date is stored."
- **S7-R2**: "A "day of the following month" term is always due in the month after the invoice date, whatever the day of the invoice: with day 10, an invoice dated Oct 5 and one dated Oct 28 are both due Nov 10."
- **S7-R3**: "When the following month is shorter than the term's day, the due date is that month's last day: day 31 invoiced in October is due Nov 30; day 30 or 31 invoiced in January is due Feb 28 (Feb 29 in a leap year); day 31 invoiced in March is due Apr 30."
- **S7-R4**: "An invoice dated in December on a "day of the following month" term is due in January of the next year (day 10, invoice Dec 15 2026, due Jan 10 2027)."
- **S7-R5**: "COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold are due on the invoice date, as today."
- **S7-R6**: "Due dates are counted on the calendar of the location the invoice or vendor invoice belongs to, never the location of the user, API connection or import that creates or views it, so a daylight saving change inside the span never moves the date: an Edmonton invoice dated Oct 5 on "10th of the following month" is due Nov 10 (not Nov 9)."
- **S7-R6a**: "The same invoice shows the same due date to every user, whichever location they are working in. A vendor invoice created by receiving a delivery uses the delivery's location, even when the receiving user is working in another location."
- **S7-R6b**: "When the record's location has no time zone set, the due date is counted on UTC."
- **S7-R7**: "An invoice keeps the term and due date it was created with. Changing the customer's term later, deactivating the term or moving the customer never changes an existing invoice."
- **S7-R8**: "When a user changes the invoice date on an invoice (existing behavior), the due date is recalculated from the customer's term by the rules above, including the shop's own terms."
- **S7-R9**: "A vendor invoice stores no term. Its due date is calculated by the same rules from the vendor's term when the vendor invoice is created, recalculated when its invoice date or its vendor changes, and stored."
- **S8-E1**: "If QuickBooks already has a term with the same name but a different rule, QuickBooks keeps its own rule for that term (S8-R4). Each invoice still carries its own due date, so invoices stay correct; only the QuickBooks customer's term may differ."
- **S8-N1**: "If the QuickBooks integration is not connected, nothing is sent and Story 3: Create or edit a credit term, Story 4: Move Customers, Story 5: New Customer and Edit Customer and Story 7: Due dates behave exactly as they do without the integration."
- **S8-N2**: "A term the shop created but nobody has used on a synced record is never sent to QuickBooks."
- **S8-R1**: "When a record is sent to QuickBooks with a term QuickBooks does not have, ShopView first creates the term in QuickBooks under the same name, then sends the record. This is how the standard terms already work today."
- **S8-R2**: "A "Due Net X days" term is created in QuickBooks as a standard term due in X days."
- **S8-R3**: "A "Due day of the following month" term is created in QuickBooks as a date-driven term due on day X of the following month, so QuickBooks dates an invoice dated Oct 5 or Oct 28 with day 10 as due Nov 10, matching ShopView."
- **S8-R4**: "A term already in QuickBooks under the same name is used as it is; ShopView never changes a term in QuickBooks."
- **S8-R5**: "Each invoice carries its own term and due date to QuickBooks, as today. A vendor invoice is sent to QuickBooks as a bill with its stored due date (S7-R9) and the vendor's term at the moment it is sent."
- **S8-R6**: "Changing a customer's term in ShopView, by Edit Customer or by Move Customers (Story 4: Move Customers), does not change the customer record in QuickBooks, exactly as an Edit Customer save does not today. The QuickBooks customer keeps the term it was created with; new invoices still carry the right term."
- **S8-R7**: "Deactivating, reactivating, editing or deleting a term in ShopView sends nothing to QuickBooks. A term already created in QuickBooks stays there."
- **S8-R8**: "AccountingHub and IBS are unchanged: both receive each invoice's or vendor invoice's due date only, never the term name, so a shop's own term names never reach them."
- **S9-E1**: "A customer on one of the shop's own terms is treated like a Net customer: the warning shows when their balance exceeds a limit above $0."
- **S9-E2**: "A customer on Credit Hold is still blocked from invoicing as today; only the Over Credit warning is removed for them."
- **S9-N1**: "The warning and the badge never show when the customer's credit limit is $0 or blank."
- **S9-N2**: "The warning and the badge never show for a customer on COD, Due on Receipt or Credit Hold, whatever their limit or balance. A customer with a blank term counts as COD."
- **S9-R1**: "When a user opens a new work order for a customer whose balance exceeds a credit limit above $0, and the customer is not on COD, Due on Receipt or Credit Hold, the existing confirmation shows unchanged: title "Confirmation", message "Are you sure you want to open a new work order? This customer is over their credit limit.", buttons "Create" and "Cancel"."
- **S9-R2**: "The "Over limit" badge on work orders and part sales follows the same rule as S9-R1."
- **S9-R3**: "Wording and buttons are unchanged."
- **S9-R4**: "Opening a part sale shows no over-credit confirmation, as today; for part sales only the 'Over limit' badge follows S9-R1."
- **A-1500** (Assumptions): "A single term can hold more than 1,500 customers (the test organization holds 1,528 on Due on Receipt and 768 vendors on COD); the Credit Terms page and the Move Customers window are sized for that."
- **A-SPELL** (Assumptions): "Stored customer and vendor terms carry inconsistent spellings from old imports and migrations (for example "NET 30" and "Due On Receipt"); every count, match and move treats names as equal when they differ only in capitals and spaces."
- **EH-S10** (Story 10, Error Handling): "None new. Import and API errors are unchanged."
- **EH-S2** (Story 2, Error Handling): "If a save is refused, the app's standard error alert shows the reason, using these exact texts: "This is your default for new customers. Pick another default first." (deactivating or deleting the default); "Used by {holders}. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." (editing or deleting a term in use); "Always available. COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold can't be deactivated."; "Pick an active term other than Credit Hold." (making an inactive term or Credit Hold the default); "Credit term not found." (the term was deleted meanwhile). The delete confirmation stays open when the delete is refused."
- **EH-S3a** (Story 3, Error Handling): "If Create or Save is refused by the server, the app's standard error alert shows the reason (the S3-R9 texts, or "Pick Due Net X days or Due day of the following month." when no option is sent, or "Used by {holders}. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." when the term became in use meanwhile). The window stays open with the user's input."
- **EH-S3b** (Story 3, Error Handling): "On Create, a success toast shows "{name} created". On Save Changes, a success toast shows "{name} saved". Both are the same green toast as the page's other actions."
- **EH-S4a** (Story 4, Error Handling): "If every ticked customer has left the term before the confirm, the move is refused and the app's standard error alert shows "Pick at least one customer to move." The window stays open."
- **EH-S4b** (Story 4, Error Handling): "If the chosen "Move To" term was deactivated or deleted meanwhile, the move is refused with "Pick an active term other than {from term}." The window stays open."
- **EH-S5a** (Story 5, Error Handling): "If the chosen term was deactivated or deleted after the form opened, Save on New Customer is refused and the app's standard error alert shows "Pick an active credit term." The form stays open."
- **EH-S6** (Story 6, Error Handling): "No new messages. Vendor save errors are unchanged."
- **EH-S8** (Story 8, Error Handling): "If QuickBooks refuses to create the term or the record, the record lands on the QB Unexported report with the reason QuickBooks gave, the existing failure path, and can be resent from there once fixed. ShopView work is never blocked."
- **EH-S9** (Story 9, Error Handling): "None. No new messages."
- **FO-VENDOR-COD** (Feature Overview): "Customers and vendors share one term list. The shop's default applies to new customers only; new vendors keep starting on COD."
- **GOAL-1** (Goals): "A user with 'Settings → Finance' can set the default term for new customers to any active term except Credit Hold, and every customer created afterwards in ShopView, by contacts import or by the Public API without a term starts on it."
- **GOAL-2** (Goals): "A shop can create a "Net X days" or "day of the following month" term and select it on any customer or vendor; invoices and vendor invoices dated afterwards show the due date the term defines."
- **GOAL-3** (Goals): "Creating a customer on a non-default term takes zero extra screens: the term is set on New Customer itself."
- **GOAL-4** (Goals): "Moving any number of customers from one term to another takes one window and one confirm, plus one confirmation when the move sets credit limits to $0."
- **GOAL-5** (Goals): "The Over Credit warning never appears for a customer on COD, Due on Receipt or Credit Hold, or with a $0 or blank credit limit."
- **GOAL-6** (Goals): "With no setup at all, existing shops behave exactly as before release (default COD, the same twelve terms), and new shops start on Due on Receipt."
- **KD-ANYACTIVE** (Key Decisions): "The default can be any active term except Credit Hold."
- **KD-CONFIRM** (Key Decisions): "Deleting a term asks for confirmation; deactivating, reactivating and making a term the default are confirmed only where the result is not obvious. Delete is permanent and asks first. Deactivate and Reactivate are reversible and act at once with a toast. Make Default confirms, because its effect on existing customers (none) needs saying."
- **KD-DEACT** (Key Decisions): "Deactivating a term hides it from the customer and vendor pickers only. Everyone on it keeps it, shown as "{name} (Inactive)" on their card and in Edit, until someone picks another term; invoices print the plain name; it can be reactivated at any time."
- **KD-DEFAULT** (Key Decisions): "The current default cannot be deactivated or deleted; the action is shown disabled with the reason."
- **KD-EXIST** (Key Decisions): "Existing organizations keep COD as their default at release; organizations created after release start on Due on Receipt. No existing customer changes term."
- **KD-INLINE** (Key Decisions): "The page explains every term inline in a Due column; only COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold carry an info tooltip."
- **KD-LENIENT** (Key Decisions): "Imports and the Public API stay lenient. A blank term gets the default; a name matching any of the shop's terms (standard or its own, active or deactivated, ignoring capitals and spaces) is saved as that term in its correct spelling, and a deactivated one then shows as "{name} (Inactive)"; any other text is saved as sent and dated as today."
- **KD-LOCK** (Key Decisions): "Once a term is in use (by any customer, vendor or invoice), its name and days are locked; it can be deactivated but not deleted. An unused term can be edited or deleted."
- **KD-LOG** (Key Decisions): "Every change on the Credit Terms page is recorded in an organization-wide Credit Terms Log (Story 12: Credit Terms audit log), and every customer moved by Move Customers also gets a note naming the old term, the new term and the user."
- **KD-MODAL** (Key Decisions): "Departure: the New Credit Term and Move Customers windows do not close on Escape or a click outside."
- **KD-MOVEGATE** (Key Decisions): "Departure: Move Customers keeps a gate from another area on a Settings page. It needs 'Customers → Create & Edit' and 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' as well as 'Settings → Finance'."
- **KD-MOVEZERO** (Key Decisions): "Moving customers to COD or Credit Hold sets their credit limits to $0 only after a confirmation, and keeps each previous limit in that customer's note."
- **KD-NAMES** (Key Decisions): "Shops name their terms freely; the Due column always shows the rule. A term named "Net 6" may be due on the 25th of the following month (S3-E1)."
- **KD-NETDEACT** (Key Decisions): "Any other standard term (Net 7 to Net 120) can be deactivated to shorten the pickers."
- **KD-NEWONLY** (Key Decisions): "A changed default applies to new customers only; moving existing customers is a separate, explicit action (Move Customers)."
- **KD-NOFLAG** (Key Decisions): "No feature flag. The feature ships to everyone; release-day behavior (Story 11: Release day) leaves existing shops exactly as they were until they act."
- **KD-ONELIST** (Key Decisions): "One default and one term list per organization, shared by customers and vendors."
- **KD-OWNNET** (Key Decisions): "The shop's own terms behave like Net terms: Charge Account allowed, invoicing never blocked."
- **KD-RANGE** (Key Decisions): "Net terms run from 1 to 365 days."
- **KD-TABLE** (Key Decisions): "The page is one table with Active and Inactive tabs, the default picked from each row's menu."
- **KD-THREE** (Key Decisions): "COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold can never be deactivated."
- **KD-ZERO** (Key Decisions): "COD and Credit Hold customers have a $0 credit limit that cannot be changed, in New Customer and Edit Customer, and imports and the Public API save them with $0 (S10-R6, S10-R8)."
- **KD-ZERO2** (Key Decisions): "The rule applies only to customers created or edited after release: an existing COD or Credit Hold customer with a limit stored from before keeps it until the customer is next edited in Edit Customer, updated by the contacts import, or updated through the Public API with a credit term or credit limit or moved to COD or Credit Hold by Move Customers; each of those saves $0."
- **OOS-1** (Out of Scope): "A separate default term for new vendors. New vendors keep starting on COD. Whether vendors get their own default is a follow-up decision (open question on the review page)."
- **OOS-10** (Out of Scope): "Statements, the customer portal and A/R aging gain no new screens and show no credit term name on their own screens (an invoice document opened from the portal prints the term as any printed invoice does); see S11-R14 for the due dates they recalculate. They show stored due dates, except where one is recalculated today: the A/R dashboard for records with no stored due date, and credit memo due dates on statements and the unpaid transactions list. Those recalculated dates are counted on the calendar of the record's own location (S7-R6)."
- **OOS-11** (Out of Scope): "A bulk term change from the Customers list. Move Customers lives on the Credit Terms page only, where the term's full customer list and the default already are. A Customers-list bulk change is a possible follow-up."
- **OOS-12** (Out of Scope): "A change history for single customers and vendors. A term or credit limit changed one record at a time (New or Edit Customer, Edit Vendor, imports, the Public API) leaves no visible record, exactly as today; only Move Customers writes a note on each moved customer (S4-R11) and a Credit Terms Log row. A customer and vendor audit log is a later Founder Mode item."
- **OOS-14** (Out of Scope): "AccountingHub and IBS are unchanged: both receive each invoice's or vendor invoice's due date only, never the term name, so a shop's own term names never reach them (S8-R8)."
- **OOS-2** (Out of Scope): "Merging with AccountingHub's own Payment Terms list. AccountingHub keeps its separate Payment Terms list (Accounting Settings). A merge waits until AccountingHub's term rules are confirmed (follow-up, open question on the review page)."
- **OOS-3** (Out of Scope): "Early-payment discount terms (for example "2% 10 Net 30"). 5 insights, mostly vendor-side; not a "Net X" or "day of the following month" term. Not planned."
- **OOS-4** (Out of Scope): "Per-location or per-work-type default terms. 2 insights. One default per organization."
- **OOS-5** (Out of Scope): "Updating the customer's term inside QuickBooks after it was created there. No such sync exists today; building one is new scope."
- **OOS-6** (Out of Scope): "Renaming or changing the days of a term in use. QuickBooks cannot rename a term and invoices hold the name. To rename, deactivate the term and create a new one."
- **OOS-7** (Out of Scope): "Editing, renaming or deleting the twelve standard terms. Their names are already on every shop's invoices and in every connected QuickBooks file, and QuickBooks cannot rename a term; shops that want different wording create their own term."
- **OOS-9** (Out of Scope): "Vendor credit limits. Vendor credit limits behave exactly as today on every term, including COD and Credit Hold. A vendor's limit plays no part in invoicing customers or in the Over Credit warning, so the customer $0 rule has no problem to solve there (owning PM, 2026-10-20)."
- **PERM-DEFAULTS** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "By default 'Settings → Finance' is on for the Admin profile (required, cannot be turned off), Parts Manager and Office User, and off for Service Manager, Senior Service Advisor, Service Advisor, Foreman, Technician, Parts Technician, Sales Representative and Time Clock User."
- **PERM-FIELDS** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "The Credit Terms and Credit Limit fields on New Customer and Edit Customer, and the Credit Terms field on New Vendor and Edit Vendor, are governed by 'View and Manage AP/AR Data', as the existing Edit fields are."
- **PERM-IGNORED** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "A user without 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' who creates or edits a customer has any term or limit they send ignored by the server (S5-N4)."
- **PERM-LIST** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "The organization's credit term list itself (the terms that fill those dropdowns, and which one is the default) loads for every user in the shop, as today,"
- **PERM-MOVE** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "Move Customers additionally needs 'Customers → Create & Edit' and 'View and Manage AP/AR Data', because it changes customer records;"
- **PERM-NONEW** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "No new permission is introduced."
- **PERM-OOTB** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "Out of the box, Move Customers is therefore available to Admin, Parts Manager and Office User; Service Manager, Senior Service Advisor and Sales Representative hold 'Customers → Create & Edit' and 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' but not 'Settings → Finance', so they cannot open the Credit Terms page."
- **PERM-PAGE** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "Viewing and using the Credit Terms page (the list, New Credit Term, Edit, Make Default, Deactivate, Reactivate, Delete) is governed by 'Settings → Finance', the same permission as Payment Methods."
- **PERM-SCREEN** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "On the Roles & Permissions screen these appear as "Finance" (a sub-row under "Settings" in the page-access toggles), the "Customers" row's "Create & Edit" column, and the "View and Manage AP/AR Data" cross toggle."
- **T-COUNTER** (Terminology): "Counter Sale customer → The organization's built-in walk-in customer that over-the-counter sales are invoiced to; there is exactly one per organization, created with the organization. It always stays on COD and is never counted, listed or moved on the Credit Terms page."
- **T-DEFAULT** (Terminology): "Default (default credit term) → The term a new customer starts on. It never changes an existing customer."
- **T-INACTIVE** (Terminology): "Inactive → A deactivated term: absent from the customer and vendor pickers, still held by whoever had it, and shown as "{name} (Inactive)" on their card and in Edit."
- **T-INUSE** (Terminology): "In use → A term is in use when at least one customer, vendor or invoice (work order or part sale invoice) holds it. Vendor invoices, deliveries and vendor credits store no term, so they never count. The Counter Sale customer is never counted. A customer or vendor with a blank term counts as COD. Every invoice ever created counts, including reversed and paid ones; estimates never count. Customers and vendors count and are listed whatever their status."
- **T-MDVSMC** (Terminology): "Make Default vs Move Customers → Make Default changes only which term new customers start on. Move Customers changes the term of existing customers the user picks. Neither does the other's job."
- **T-OWN** (Terminology): "The shop's own term → A term the shop created on the Credit Terms page, of one of two kinds: Due Net X days (due X days after the invoice date) or Due day of the following month (due on day X of the month after the invoice date)."
- **T-STANDARD** (Terminology): "Standard term → One of the twelve terms every organization has: COD, Due on Receipt, Net 7, Net 15, Net 20, Net 30, Net 45, Net 50, Net 60, Net 90, Net 120, Credit Hold."
- **T-VI** (Terminology): "Vendor invoice → The app's word for a bill received from a vendor. QuickBooks calls the same record a bill; this PRD says "bill" only when describing what appears inside QuickBooks."
- **UFS-10** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover Deactivate / Reactivate | "Hide from customer and vendor pickers" / "Show in pickers again" | Tooltip on hover"
- **UFS-11** (User Feedback Summary): "Make Default chosen | "Make {name} the default?" / "New customers start on {name}. Customers you already have keep their current term." | Confirmation, buttons "Cancel" and "Make Default""
- **UFS-12** (User Feedback Summary): "Make Default confirmed | "{name} is now the default" / "New customers start on {name}." | Success toast with a Close action"
- **UFS-13** (User Feedback Summary): "Term deactivated | "{name} deactivated" | Success toast"
- **UFS-14** (User Feedback Summary): "Term reactivated | "{name} reactivated" | Success toast"
- **UFS-15** (User Feedback Summary): "Delete Credit Term chosen | "Are you sure you want to delete this credit term?" | Confirmation titled "Confirmation", buttons "Cancel" and red "Delete Credit Term""
- **UFS-16** (User Feedback Summary): "Term deleted | "{name} deleted" | Success toast"
- **UFS-17** (User Feedback Summary): "Credit term created | "{name} created" | Success toast"
- **UFS-18** (User Feedback Summary): "Credit term edited and saved | "{name} saved" | Success toast"
- **UFS-19** (User Feedback Summary): "Name field hint | "Up to 31 characters. Prints on invoices and goes to QuickBooks." | Hint under the field, always shown"
- **UFS-2** (User Feedback Summary): "Inactive tab is empty | "No inactive terms" | Inline line in place of the table"
- **UFS-20** (User Feedback Summary): "Name empty | "Enter a name." | Inline error, persists until corrected"
- **UFS-22** (User Feedback Summary): "Name matches a standard term | "{name} is already a standard term. Pick another name." | Inline error, persists until corrected"
- **UFS-23** (User Feedback Summary): "Name matches one of the shop's terms | "A term called {name} already exists." | Inline error, persists until corrected"
- **UFS-24** (User Feedback Summary): "Days out of range | "Enter 1 to 365 days." | Inline error, clears as soon as the number is valid"
- **UFS-25** (User Feedback Summary): "Day out of range | "Enter a day from 1 to 31." | Inline error, clears as soon as the number is valid"
- **UFS-27** (User Feedback Summary): "Valid Net term typed | "{Name} means payment is due {X} days after the invoice date. An invoice dated {today} is due {due}." | Blue information line, updates as the user types"
- **UFS-28** (User Feedback Summary): "Valid day-of-month term typed | "{Name} means payment is due on the {ordinal X} of the following month. An invoice dated {today} is due {due}." | Blue information line, updates as the user types"
- **UFS-29** (User Feedback Summary): "Edit or delete of a term in use refused (server) | "Used by {holders}. Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." | Standard error alert"
- **UFS-3** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover the lock on COD, Due on Receipt or Credit Hold | "Always available." / "COD, Due on Receipt and Credit Hold can't be deactivated." | Tooltip on hover"
- **UFS-30** (User Feedback Summary): "Deactivate or delete of the default refused (server) | "This is your default for new customers. Pick another default first." | Standard error alert"
- **UFS-32** (User Feedback Summary): "Make Default of an inactive term or Credit Hold refused (server) | "Pick an active term other than Credit Hold." | Standard error alert"
- **UFS-33** (User Feedback Summary): "Term no longer exists (server) | "Credit term not found." | Standard error alert"
- **UFS-34** (User Feedback Summary): "Move To hint | "Prefilled with your default for new customers." | Hint under Move To while the default is selected"
- **UFS-35** (User Feedback Summary): "Move To is COD or Credit Hold | "Their credit limit will be set to $0." | Line under Move To while COD or Credit Hold is selected"
- **UFS-36** (User Feedback Summary): "Move to COD or Credit Hold pressed | "Set {n} customers' credit limit to $0?" ("Set 1 customer's credit limit to $0?") / "This can't be undone. Their current limits are kept in each customer's note, and the Credit Terms Log records the move." | Confirmation, buttons "Cancel" (back to the window, choices kept) and red "Set Limits to $0 and Move" (displayed "Set Limits To $0 And Move")"
- **UFS-37** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover a greyed Move Customers (missing permission) | "You need Customers → Create & Edit and View and Manage AP/AR Data to move customers." | Tooltip on hover, item greyed"
- **UFS-39** (User Feedback Summary): "Move Customers search has no match | "No customers match your search." | Inline line in the list area"
- **UFS-4** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover the info icon on COD | "Cash on delivery. COD customers can't charge to account, so payment is taken when the invoice is created." | Tooltip on hover"
- **UFS-40** (User Feedback Summary): "Customers moved | "{n} customers moved to {term}." / "1 customer moved to {term}." | Success toast"
- **UFS-41** (User Feedback Summary): "Note on each moved customer | "Credit term changed from {old term} to {new term} by {user name} (Move Customers)." followed by "Credit limit {old limit} → $0." when the move changed the limit | Note on the customer's Notes"
- **UFS-42** (User Feedback Summary): "Move with no ticked customer still on the term (server) | "Pick at least one customer to move." | Standard error alert, window stays open"
- **UFS-43** (User Feedback Summary): "Move To term no longer active (server) | "Pick an active term other than {from term}." | Standard error alert, window stays open"
- **UFS-44** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover the locked Credit Limit (COD or Credit Hold) | "COD and Credit Hold customers can't charge to account, so they have no credit limit." | Tooltip on hover"
- **UFS-46** (User Feedback Summary): "New Customer term no longer active (server) | "Pick an active credit term." | Standard error alert, form stays open"
- **UFS-47** (User Feedback Summary): "Page "⋯" menu item | "Audit Log" | Menu item, opens the "Credit Terms Log" window"
- **UFS-48** (User Feedback Summary): "Credit Terms Log events | "Credit term created", "Credit term edited", "Credit term deactivated", "Credit term reactivated", "Credit term deleted", "Default changed", "Customers moved" | Event column text, one row per saved change"
- **UFS-49** (User Feedback Summary): "Credit Terms Log details | "{name}: {rule}" (created, deactivated, reactivated, deleted); "Name: {old} → {new}" and/or "Due: {old rule} → {new rule}" (edited); "{old} → {new}" (default changed); "{n} customers from {from} to {to}", plus "Credit limit set to $0 (previous limits are in each customer's note)" when the target is COD or Credit Hold (customers moved) | Details column text"
- **UFS-5** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover the info icon on Due on Receipt | "Payment is due on the invoice date. You can charge the invoice to the customer's account instead of taking payment." | Tooltip on hover"
- **UFS-50** (User Feedback Summary): "Credit Terms Log has no rows | "No history here. Yet." | Empty state in the table"
- **UFS-51** (User Feedback Summary): "Credit Terms Log fails to load | "Something went wrong loading the Credit Terms Log. Please try again." | Standard error alert; the table shows no rows and repeats the same sentence in place of the empty-state line"
- **UFS-52** (User Feedback Summary): "Over credit limit on New Work Order (unchanged wording) | "Are you sure you want to open a new work order? This customer is over their credit limit." | Confirmation titled "Confirmation", buttons "Create" and "Cancel""
- **UFS-6** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover the info icon on Credit Hold | "No invoices can be created for a customer on Credit Hold until their credit term is changed." | Tooltip on hover"
- **UFS-7** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover a blocked Edit or Delete Credit Term on a term in use | "Used by {holders}" / "Deactivate it instead to take it out of the pickers." | Tooltip on hover, item greyed"
- **UFS-8** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover a blocked Deactivate or Delete Credit Term on the default | "This is your default for new customers" / "Pick another default first." | Tooltip on hover, item greyed"
- **UFS-9** (User Feedback Summary): "Hover a blocked Move Customers | "No customers are on this term" | Tooltip on hover, item greyed"
- **Jira SV-11672 acceptance criterion**: "Given the name "  Net   14 " is saved, then it saves as "Net 14" (S3-R12)"
- **Jira SV-11673 acceptance criterion**: "Given a customer with a $500 limit moved to COD, then its limit is $0 and its note reads "Credit term changed from {old term} to COD by {user name} (Move Customers). Credit limit $500.00 → $0." (S4-R11, S4-R16, S4-R20)."
- **Jira SV-11674 acceptance criterion**: "Given a COD customer with a pre-release limit of $1,000, when Edit Customer opens, then the limit shows $0.00, disabled, and saving saves $0 (S5-R9)."
- **Jira SV-11680 acceptance criterion**: "Given customers stored as "NET 30", then the stored text is kept, they count under Net 30 on the page and their card shows "NET 30" (S11-E2)."
- **Jira SV-11681 acceptance criterion**: "Given the default changes from COD to Net 14, then a row reads "Default changed" / "COD → Net 14" (S12-R3)."
- **Story 5, Form factor**: "Form factor: Mobile-optimized: Credit Terms and Credit Limit sit side by side at every width, as the window already pairs City / ZIP and State / Country; on a touch screen, tapping the locked Credit Limit shows the explanation under the field and tapping again hides it."
- **Story 9, Form factor**: "Form factor: Mobile-optimized: no new screen; the existing confirmation and "Over limit" badge keep their existing mobile variants and only change when they appear."

## 11. Open questions (Chris Ward, drafted 23 Oct 2026, NOT sent)
- Q1: Story 11 (release day) jumps from requirement 14 to requirement 16. Is a requirement missing there? — held cases: none
- Q2: The Credit Terms list puts "any other term A to Z" between the day-of-the-following-month terms and Credit Hold. Which terms are these, when a shop can only create Net terms and day-of-the-following-month terms? — held cases: none
- Q3: If another user RENAMES a term (or changes its days) after my Credit Terms page loaded, and I then use that term's menu (for example Make Default or Delete), what should I see? — held cases: S2-36
- Q4: Where else a customer's credit term is shown (the A/R Collection call list "Credit term:", the customer on a work order or part sale), should a deactivated term read "Net 30 (Inactive)" or just "Net 30"? — held cases: S11-20
- Q5: When customers are moved off a deactivated term, does the note on each customer and the Credit Terms Log name the old term plainly ("from Net 60") or with "(Inactive)" ("from Net 60 (Inactive)")? — held cases: S4-35
- Q6: A customer stored with an old spelling such as "NET 30" is moved off Net 30. Does its note say "from NET 30" (what was stored) or "from Net 30" (the term's spelling)? — held cases: S4-36
- Q7: An invoice was created when the customer was on Net 30. Later the customer is moved to Net 60, and then someone changes that invoice's date. Is the new due date counted with Net 30 (the invoice's own term) or Net 60 (the customer's term now)? — held cases: S7-10
